     429:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 3);
     430:   const results = new Array(arr.length);

Linha 434

     429:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 3);

     432: 
     433:   async function worker() {
>>   434:     while (idx < arr.length) {
     435:       const current = idx++;
     436:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
     437:     }
     438:   }
     439: 
     440:   await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, worker));
     441:   return results;
     442: }

     180: function mergeLateLists(lists, baseYmd) {

     191:         lastYmd: r?.lastYmd || null,
     192:         // ✅ normaliza aqui (evita “09h/09HS/9”)
     193:         lastCloseHour: r?.lastCloseHour ? normalizeHourLike(r.lastCloseHour) : "",
     194:       };

     468: function dedupeDraws(list, scopeKey, ymd) {

     167:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
     168:   return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
     169: }

     494: async function runPool(items, worker, concurrency = 3) {
     495:   const queue = [...items];
     496:   const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, concurrency) }, async () => {
>>   497:     while (queue.length) {
     498:       const it = queue.shift();
     499:       // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
     500:       await worker(it);
     501:     }
     502:   });
     503:   await Promise.all(runners);
     504: }

     253: function addDaysYMD(ymd, deltaDays) {
     254:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return ymd;
     255:   const [Y, M, D] = ymd.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
     256:   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D);
     257:   dt.setDate(dt.getDate() + Number(deltaDays || 0));
     258:   return `${dt.getFullYear()}-${pad2(dt.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(dt.getDate())}`;
